Ruling: model error; i1040gi p. 18, Step 2, question 1, and p. 19, Step 4, question 2, "Was your qualifying relative a U.S. citizen, U.S. national, U.S. resident alien, or a resident of Canada or Mexico? ... No. STOP ... You can't claim this person as a dependent"; p501 p. 21, Multiple Support Agreement, "two or more persons, each of whom would be able to claim the person as a dependent but for the support test, together provide more than half of the person's support" (the support test of a qualifying relative; i1040gi p. 19, Step 4, "For whom you provided over half of the person's support in 2025. But see ... Multiple support agreements"). New: the reply drops the citizenship test from its dependency tests: 200 completed values, project no and candidate yes, every one on an entry that is neither a U.S. citizen, national or resident alien nor a resident of Canada or Mexico (192 qualifying relatives, for example classm.s2.3.deepseek dep_c, a 22-year-old child; 8 qualifying children, for example classm.f8889.1.deepseek dep_c). It also drops the "Could you be claimed as a dependent" test (sa.dependent; Step 2, question 4, and Step 4, question 5) and the joint-return spouse-relative route (dependent.spouse_relative), with no completed witness. As the earlier ruling, it refuses dependent.multiple_support for every entry (120 completed unknowns on qualifying children, for example classm.f4952.1.deepseek dep_c; generated 126 dep_b refused) and asks dependent.lawful of household_member entries (14, for example classm.clcw26.2.deepseek dep_b). Its new part-year residency route asks dependent.born_died where the months answer already applies the birth and death rule (1 completed, mutation.t2.qss_sdtw_sli dep_a). Its earlier joint_return question on qualifying relatives is gone. executable-triage-v2 (`reviews/2025/transcription/triage/dependent.deepseek.json`; 1,638 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 2,358 partial records; 7,537 comparisons): 6,234 of 7,537 agree; 483 candidate-unknown, 338 value, 316 project-refuses, 161 project-unknown, 5 candidate-refuses; on returns the full interview completes: 200 value, 155 candidate-unknown, 3 project-unknown, 1 candidate-refuses. Every completed-return difference was enumerated with the witness cap lifted (scratch `/tmp/r2/adj/dep/full/dependent.deepseek.json`). Project stands.

Ruling: model error; i1040gi p. 7, "If you are filing a joint return, only one spouse is required to have a valid SSN in order to be eligible for the CTC and ACTC. The other spouse must have either an SSN or ITIN"; p. 18, Step 3, question 4, "Did you, or your spouse if filing a joint return, and this child have SSNs valid for employment"; Step 2, question 4. As the earlier ruling (the reply does not test household.ctc_ssn): it now tests household.taxpayer_work_ssn, the filer's own SSN only, and no other person's identification number: stage13.ctc_spouse_ssn (only the spouse has an SSN valid for employment: project true, candidate false; likewise generated 383), stage13.ctc_spouse_no_id (the spouse has no SSN or ITIN: project false, candidate true); and reading its qualifying-child test directly instead of dependent.claimed it drops the "Could you be claimed as a dependent" test (sa.dependent yes: stage8.single.filer_is_dependent, generated 46 dep_a and others, project false, candidate true). These 12 values are on incomplete or refused records; on completed returns only partial records and one born_died question (mutation.t2.qss_sdtw_sli dep_a) differ. executable-triage-v2 (`reviews/2025/transcription/triage/dependent.deepseek.json`; 1,638 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 2,358 partial records; 7,537 comparisons): 7,361 of 7,537 agree; 97 candidate-unknown, 38 project-unknown, 28 project-refuses, 12 value, 1 candidate-refuses; on returns the full interview completes: 6 candidate-unknown. Every completed-return difference was enumerated with the witness cap lifted (scratch `/tmp/r2/adj/dep/full/dependent.deepseek.json`). Project stands.

Ruling: model error; i2441 p. 1, Qualifying Person(s), "1. A qualifying child under age 13 whom you can claim as a dependent." The earlier ruling's defect is gone (the reply now tests f2441.care), but its under-13 route asks only that the person be claimed, not that they be your qualifying child: 135 values, project no and candidate yes, on qualifying relatives under 13 with care paid (for example classm.dependent.3.deepseek and classm.dependent.4.deepseek dep_a), all on refused returns. On completed returns only partial records differ. No completed-return value differs. executable-triage-v2 (`reviews/2025/transcription/triage/dependent.deepseek.json`; 1,638 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 2,358 partial records; 7,537 comparisons): 7,099 of 7,537 agree; 246 candidate-unknown, 135 value, 52 candidate-refuses, 3 project-unknown, 2 project-refuses; on returns the full interview completes: 5 candidate-unknown. Every completed-return difference was enumerated with the witness cap lifted (scratch `/tmp/r2/adj/dep/full/dependent.deepseek.json`). Project stands.

Ruling: model error; f8863 p. 2, line 31; i8863 p. 2, "You may be able to claim an education credit if you, your spouse, or a dependent you claim on your tax return was a student"; p970 p. 11. As the earlier ruling: no gate on the student being your claimed dependent, so it asks dependent.edu of every entry (880 completed unknowns where the project hides the line, for example classm.f4952.1.deepseek dep_a); and, as this project's ruling on the Claude family's same reading, it requires dependent.edu_tin_issued (86 completed, for example classm.f8863.2.deepseek dep_a), against the p970 p. 11 rule for a timely ITIN or ATIN application. Its edu_over refusal is gone; the project's refusals are its cited edu_over_refused screen. executable-triage-v2 (`reviews/2025/transcription/triage/dependent.deepseek.json`; 1,638 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 2,358 partial records; 7,537 comparisons): 4,322 of 7,537 agree; 3,105 candidate-unknown, 73 project-refuses, 37 project-unknown; on returns the full interview completes: 967 candidate-unknown. Every completed-return difference was enumerated with the witness cap lifted (scratch `/tmp/r2/adj/dep/full/dependent.deepseek.json`). Project stands.

## Raw second transcription

```yaml
lines:
  - id: dependent.dc_line2d
    printed: "2(d)"
    label: "Qualified expenses you incurred and paid in 2025 for the person listed in column (a)."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: dependent.dc_paid}
    shown_when: dependent.dc_qp
    note: "The supplied dc_paid is taken as the column (d) amount; the return-level Part III benefit subtraction is handled by Form 2441."
  - id: dependent.dc_line2c
    printed: "2(c)"
    label: "Check here if the qualifying person was over age 12 and was disabled."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - op: test
          eq: [dependent.dc_incapable, {const: true}]
        - op: test
          any:
            - op: test
              all:
                - op: test
                  gt: [dependent.age, {param: dc.child_age}]
                - op: test
                  gt: [dependent.dc_paid, {const: 0}]
            - op: test
              all:
                - op: test
                  eq: [dependent.age, {param: dc.child_age}]
                - op: test
                  eq: [dependent.dc_over12_care, {const: true}]
    shown_when: dependent.dc_qp
    note: "When age is exactly 13, the dc_over12_care answer controls. For younger ages the box is false. For older ages it is true when disabled care expenses were paid."
  - id: dependent.edu_line27
    printed: "27"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses (see instructions). Don't enter more than $4,000."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_aoc_ok, {const: true}]}
          then: {def: aqee, bind: {inst: dependent.edu_inst, mat: dependent.edu_mat, adj: dependent.h_edu_adj}, line: line27}
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_aoc_ok, {const: false}]}
          then: dependent.h_zero
    shown_when: dependent.h_edu_aoc_ok
  - id: dependent.edu_line28
    printed: "28"
    label: "Subtract $2,000 from line 27. If zero or less, enter -0-."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_aoc_ok, {const: true}]}
          then: {def: aqee, bind: {inst: dependent.edu_inst, mat: dependent.edu_mat, adj: dependent.h_edu_adj}, line: line28}
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_aoc_ok, {const: false}]}
          then: dependent.h_zero
    shown_when: dependent.h_edu_aoc_ok
  - id: dependent.edu_line29
    printed: "29"
    label: "Multiply line 28 by 25% (0.25)."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_aoc_ok, {const: true}]}
          then: {def: aqee, bind: {inst: dependent.edu_inst, mat: dependent.edu_mat, adj: dependent.h_edu_adj}, line: line29}
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_aoc_ok, {const: false}]}
          then: dependent.h_zero
    shown_when: dependent.h_edu_aoc_ok
  - id: dependent.edu_line30
    printed: "30"
    label: "If line 28 is zero, enter the amount from line 27. Otherwise, add $2,000 to the amount on line 29 and enter the result. Skip line 31. Include the total of all amounts from all Parts III, line 30, on Part I, line 1."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_aoc_ok, {const: true}]}
          then: {def: aqee, bind: {inst: dependent.edu_inst, mat: dependent.edu_mat, adj: dependent.h_edu_adj}, line: line30}
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_aoc_ok, {const: false}]}
          then: dependent.h_zero
    shown_when: dependent.h_edu_aoc_ok
  - id: dependent.edu_line31
    printed: "31"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses (see instructions). Include the total of all amounts from all Parts III, line 31, on Part II, line 10."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_llc_allowed, {const: true}]}
          then: {def: aqee, bind: {inst: dependent.edu_inst, mat: dependent.edu_mat, adj: dependent.h_edu_adj}, line: ws4_llc}
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_llc_allowed, {const: false}]}
          then: dependent.h_zero
    shown_when: dependent.h_edu_llc_allowed
  - id: dependent.one
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Each dependent counts as one."
    type: Count
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.one}
  - id: dependent.claimed
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This person is claimed as your dependent."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_available, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qr, {const: true}]}
    refuses: [dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.married, dependent.multiple_support]
  - id: dependent.ctc_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This dependent is a qualifying child for the child tax credit."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_available, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.tin, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.citizen, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {param: ctc.age_limit}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.work_ssn, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [household.taxpayer_work_ssn, {const: true}]}
    refuses: [dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.married]
  - id: dependent.odc_person
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This dependent qualifies for the credit for other dependents."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.tin, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.citizen, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [household.all_tin, {const: true}]}
    refuses: [dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.married, dependent.multiple_support]
  - id: dependent.ctc_amount
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Child tax credit amount for this dependent."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: true}]}
          then: dependent.h_ctc_per_child
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: false}]}
          then: dependent.h_zero
  - id: dependent.odc_amount
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Credit for other dependents amount for this dependent."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.odc_person, {const: true}]}
          then: dependent.h_odc_per_dependent
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.odc_person, {const: false}]}
          then: dependent.h_zero
  - id: dependent.actc_amount
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Additional child tax credit amount for this dependent."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: true}]}
          then: dependent.h_actc_per_child
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: false}]}
          then: dependent.h_zero
  - id: dependent.hoh_absence
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Disqualifying absence from the home for head of household purposes."
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: copy, from: dependent.absence_no_return}
  - id: dependent.hoh_test1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Head of household Test 1 parent for this person."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "parent"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.parent_home_paid, {const: true}]}
    refuses: [dependent.multiple_support]
  - id: dependent.hoh_test2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Head of household Test 2 qualifying person for this person."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_lived_half, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.hoh_absence, {const: false}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_core, {const: true}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_only_household, {const: false}]}
    refuses: [dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.married, dependent.multiple_support]
  - id: dependent.eic_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This person is a qualifying child for the earned income credit."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_eic_core, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_other_taxpayer, {const: false}]}
    refuses: [dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.married]
  - id: dependent.eic_ssn_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This EIC qualifying child has a valid SSN for EIC."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_child, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_ssn, {const: true}]}
    refuses: [dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.married]
  - id: dependent.dc_qp
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This person is a qualifying person for child and dependent care expenses."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [f2441.care, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_dc_under13, {const: true}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_incapable, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_lived_half, {const: true}]}
                - op: test
                  any:
                    - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
                    - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_dc_wouldbe, {const: true}]}
    refuses: [dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.married]
  - id: dependent.dc_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of qualifying persons for child and dependent care."
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: dependent
      field: dependent.one
      where: {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: true}]}
    note: "Return-level count over the dependent entries."
  - id: dependent.ptc_magi_counted
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This dependent's modified AGI counted for the premium tax credit, if required to file."
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ptc_files, {const: true}]}
          then: dependent.ptc_magi
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ptc_files, {const: false}]}
          then: dependent.h_zero
  - id: dependent.h_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Zero."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
  - id: dependent.h_ctc_per_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Child tax credit per qualifying child."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ctc.per_child}
  - id: dependent.h_odc_per_dependent
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Credit for other dependents per dependent."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: odc.per_dependent}
  - id: dependent.h_actc_per_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Additional child tax credit per qualifying child."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: actc.per_child}
  - id: dependent.h_qc_relationship
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualifying child relationship test."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "child"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling_descendant"}]}
  - id: dependent.h_qc_age
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualifying child age test."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.disabled, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {param: qc.age_limit}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.younger, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {param: qc.student_age_limit}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.student, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.younger, {const: true}]}
  - id: dependent.h_qc_support
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualifying child support test."
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [dependent.self_support, {const: false}]}
  - id: dependent.h_qc_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualifying child joint return test."
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [dependent.joint_return, {const: false}]}
  - id: dependent.h_qc_residency
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualifying child residency test."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, gt: [dependent.months, {const: 6, type: Count}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.kidnapped, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - op: test
              any:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.born_died, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.placed, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.part_year_home, {const: true}]}
  - id: dependent.h_other_taxpayer
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Another taxpayer can claim this person as a qualifying child."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.other_claimant, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.other_nontaxpayer, {const: false}]}
  - id: dependent.h_qc_core
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualifying child tests other than the other-claimant rule."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_relationship, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_age, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_support, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_joint, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_residency, {const: true}]}
  - id: dependent.h_qc_available
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This person is your qualifying child and no other taxpayer claims the person."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_core, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_other_taxpayer, {const: false}]}
  - id: dependent.h_qr_relationship
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualifying relative relationship or household test."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "child"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "parent"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "parent_relative"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "in_law"}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - op: test
              any:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "household_member"}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling_descendant"}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "other"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.qr_all_year, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.lawful, {const: true}]}
  - id: dependent.h_qr_gross
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualifying relative gross income test."
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, lt: [dependent.gross_income, {param: qr.gross_income_limit}]}
  - id: dependent.h_qr_support
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualifying relative support test."
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [dependent.supported, {const: true}]}
  - id: dependent.h_qr_not_qc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Not a qualifying child of you or another taxpayer."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_core, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_other_taxpayer, {const: false}]}
  - id: dependent.h_qr
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This person is your qualifying relative."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qr_relationship, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qr_gross, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qr_support, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qr_not_qc, {const: true}]}
  - id: dependent.h_lived_half
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Lived with you for more than half of 2025, including birth, death or placement rules."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, gt: [dependent.months, {const: 6, type: Count}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - op: test
              any:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.born_died, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.placed, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.part_year_home, {const: true}]}
  - id: dependent.h_only_household
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This dependent is a qualifying relative only because the person lived with you all year."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qr, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "household_member"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling_descendant"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "other"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.qr_all_year, {const: true}]}
  - id: dependent.h_eic_core
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC qualifying child tests other than the other-claimant rule."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_relationship, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_age, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc_joint, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.us_half, {const: true}]}
  - id: dependent.h_dc_under13
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This child was under age 13 for part of 2025 when care was paid."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {param: dc.child_age}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.age, {param: dc.child_age}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_under13, {const: true}]}
  - id: dependent.h_dc_wouldbe
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This person would be your dependent except for the disabled-person care exceptions."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qr_relationship, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qr_support, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qr_not_qc, {const: true}]}
  - id: dependent.h_edu_adj
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Total tax-free educational assistance and refunds allocable to these expenses."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum
      of: [dependent.edu_a2025, dependent.edu_a2026, dependent.edu_refunds]
  - id: dependent.h_edu_aoc_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This student is eligible for the American opportunity credit."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_1098t, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_ein, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_tin_issued, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line23, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line24, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line25, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line26, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, ne: [f1040.status, {const: "mfs"}]}
  - id: dependent.h_edu_llc_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This student is eligible for the lifetime learning credit."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_1098t, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_tin_issued, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, ne: [f1040.status, {const: "mfs"}]}
  - id: dependent.h_edu_llc_allowed
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This student's lifetime learning credit expenses are used instead of the American opportunity credit."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_llc_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_aoc_ok, {const: false}]}
```
